## Service: discoveryengine.googleapis.com

To call this service, we recommend that you use the Google-provided [client libraries](https://cloud.google.com/apis/docs/client-libraries-explained). If your application needs to use your own libraries to call this service, use the following information when you make the API requests.

### Discovery document

A [Discovery Document](https://developers.google.com/discovery/v1/reference/apis) is a machine-readable specification for describing and consuming REST APIs. It is used to build client libraries, IDE plugins, and other tools that interact with Google APIs. One service may provide multiple discovery documents. This service provides the following discovery documents:

  * <https://discoveryengine.googleapis.com/$discovery/rest?version=v1>
  * <https://discoveryengine.googleapis.com/$discovery/rest?version=v1beta>
  * <https://discoveryengine.googleapis.com/$discovery/rest?version=v1alpha>



### Service endpoint

A [service endpoint](https://cloud.google.com/apis/design/glossary#api_service_endpoint) is a base URL that specifies the network address of an API service. One service might have multiple service endpoints. This service has the following service endpoint and all URIs below are relative to this service endpoint:

  * `https://discoveryengine.googleapis.com`



### Regional service endpoint

A regional service endpoint is a base URL that specifies the network address of an API service in a single region. A service that is available in multiple regions might have multiple regional endpoints. Select a location to see its regional service endpoint for this service. 

global us

  

* `https://discoveryengine.googleapis.com`

## REST Resource: [v1beta.billingAccounts.billingAccountLicenseConfigs](https://docs.cloud.google.com/generative-ai-app-builder/docs/reference/rest/v1beta/billingAccounts.billingAccountLicenseConfigs)

Methods  
---  
`[distributeLicenseConfig](https://docs.cloud.google.com/generative-ai-app-builder/docs/reference/rest/v1beta/billingAccounts.billingAccountLicenseConfigs/distributeLicenseConfig)` |  `POST /v1beta/{billingAccountLicenseConfig=billingAccounts/*/billingAccountLicenseConfigs/*}:distributeLicenseConfig`   
Distributes a `[LicenseConfig](https://docs.cloud.google.com/generative-ai-app-builder/docs/reference/rest/v1beta/projects.locations.licenseConfigs#LicenseConfig)` from billing account level to project level.  
`[retractLicenseConfig](https://docs.cloud.google.com/generative-ai-app-builder/docs/reference/rest/v1beta/billingAccounts.billingAccountLicenseConfigs/retractLicenseConfig)` |  `POST /v1beta/{billingAccountLicenseConfig=billingAccounts/*/billingAccountLicenseConfigs/*}:retractLicenseConfig`   
This method is called from the billing account side to retract the `[LicenseConfig](https://docs.cloud.google.com/generative-ai-app-builder/docs/reference/rest/v1beta/projects.locations.licenseConfigs#LicenseConfig)` from the given project back to the billing account.  
  
## REST Resource: [v1beta.projects](https://docs.cloud.google.com/generative-ai-app-builder/docs/reference/rest/v1beta/projects)

Methods  
---  
`[provision](https://docs.cloud.google.com/generative-ai-app-builder/docs/reference/rest/v1beta/projects/provision)` |  `POST /v1beta/{name=projects/*}:provision`   
Provisions the project resource.  
  
## REST Resource: [v1beta.projects.locations](https://docs.cloud.google.com/generative-ai-app-builder/docs/reference/rest/v1beta/projects.locations)

Methods  
---  
`[getAclConfig](https://docs.cloud.google.com/generative-ai-app-builder/docs/reference/rest/v1beta/projects.locations/getAclConfig)` |  `GET /v1beta/{name=projects/*/locations/*/aclConfig}`   
Gets the `[AclConfig](https://docs.cloud.google.com/generative-ai-app-builder/docs/reference/rest/v1beta/AclConfig)`.  
`[getCmekConfig](https://docs.cloud.google.com/generative-ai-app-builder/docs/reference/rest/v1beta/projects.locations/getCmekConfig)` |  `GET /v1beta/{name=projects/*/locations/*/cmekConfig}`   
Gets the `[CmekConfig](https://docs.cloud.google.com/generative-ai-app-builder/docs/reference/rest/v1beta/projects.locations.cmekConfigs#CmekConfig)`.  
`[obtainCrawlRate](https://docs.cloud.google.com/generative-ai-app-builder/docs/reference/rest/v1beta/projects.locations/obtainCrawlRate)` |  `POST /v1beta/{location=projects/*/locations/*}:obtainCrawlRate`   
Obtains the time series data of organic or dedicated crawl rate for monitoring.  
`[provision](https://docs.cloud.google.com/generative-ai-app-builder/docs/reference/rest/v1beta/projects.locations/provision)` |  `POST /v1beta/{name=projects/*/locations/*}:provision`   
Provisions the project resource.  
`[removeDedicatedCrawlRate](https://docs.cloud.google.com/generative-ai-app-builder/docs/reference/rest/v1beta/projects.locations/removeDedicatedCrawlRate)` |  `POST /v1beta/{location=projects/*/locations/*}:removeDedicatedCrawlRate`   
Removes the dedicated crawl rate for a craw_rate_scope.  
`[setDedicatedCrawlRate](https://docs.cloud.google.com/generative-ai-app-builder/docs/reference/rest/v1beta/projects.locations/setDedicatedCrawlRate)` |  `POST /v1beta/{location=projects/*/locations/*}:setDedicatedCrawlRate`   
Sets the dedicated crawl rate for a crawl_rate_scope.  
`[updateAclConfig](https://docs.cloud.google.com/generative-ai-app-builder/docs/reference/rest/v1beta/projects.locations/updateAclConfig)` |  `PATCH /v1beta/{aclConfig.name=projects/*/locations/*/aclConfig}`   
Default ACL configuration for use in a location of a customer's project.  
`[updateCmekConfig](https://docs.cloud.google.com/generative-ai-app-builder/docs/reference/rest/v1beta/projects.locations/updateCmekConfig)` |  `PATCH /v1beta/{config.name=projects/*/locations/*/cmekConfig}`   
Provisions a CMEK key for use in a location of a customer's project.  
  
## REST Resource: [v1beta.projects.locations.cmekConfigs](https://docs.cloud.google.com/generative-ai-app-builder/docs/reference/rest/v1beta/projects.locations.cmekConfigs)

Methods  
---  
`[delete](https://docs.cloud.google.com/generative-ai-app-builder/docs/reference/rest/v1beta/projects.locations.cmekConfigs/delete)` |  `DELETE /v1beta/{name=projects/*/locations/*/cmekConfigs/*}`   
De-provisions a CmekConfig.  
`[get](https://docs.cloud.google.com/generative-ai-app-builder/docs/reference/rest/v1beta/projects.locations.cmekConfigs/get)` |  `GET /v1beta/{name=projects/*/locations/*/cmekConfigs/*}`

Exchanges OAuth authorization credentials for a refresh token and stores the refresh token and the scopes.  
`[buildActionInvocation](https://docs.cloud.google.com/generative-ai-app-builder/docs/reference/rest/v1alpha/projects.locations.collections.dataConnector/buildActionInvocation)` |  `POST /v1alpha/{name=projects/*/locations/*/collections/*/dataConnector}:buildActionInvocation`

**(deprecated)**` |  `GET /v1alpha/{name=projects/*/locations/*/collections/*/dataConnector}:checkRefreshToken`   
Deprecated: Checks the existence of a refresh token for the EUC user for a given connection and returns its details.

`[fetchEntitiesTypes](https://docs.cloud.google.com/generative-ai-app-builder/docs/reference/rest/v1alpha/projects.locations.collections.dataConnector/fetchEntitiesTypes)` |  `GET /v1alpha/{name=projects/*/locations/*/collections/*/dataConnector}:FetchEntitiesTypes`

## REST Resource: [v1.billingAccounts.billingAccountLicenseConfigs](https://docs.cloud.google.com/generative-ai-app-builder/docs/reference/rest/v1/billingAccounts.billingAccountLicenseConfigs)

Methods  
---

Distributes a `[LicenseConfig](https://docs.cloud.google.com/generative-ai-app-builder/docs/reference/rest/v1/projects.locations.licenseConfigs#LicenseConfig)` from billing account level to project level.  
`[retractLicenseConfig](https://docs.cloud.google.com/generative-ai-app-builder/docs/reference/rest/v1/billingAccounts.billingAccountLicenseConfigs/retractLicenseConfig)` |  `POST /v1/{billingAccountLicenseConfig=billingAccounts/*/billingAccountLicenseConfigs/*}:retractLicenseConfig`   
This method is called from the billing account side to retract the `[LicenseConfig](https://docs.cloud.google.com/generative-ai-app-builder/docs/reference/rest/v1/projects.locations.licenseConfigs#LicenseConfig)` from the given project back to the billing account.
